def calculate_tyre_dimensions(tyre_code):
    
    import schema
    it = [
        ('iso', _re_tyre_code_iso),
        ('numeric', _re_tyre_code_numeric),
        ('pax', _re_tyre_code_pax)
    ]
    for c, _r in it:
        try:
            m = _r.match(tyre_code).groupdict()
            m['code'] = c
            if c == 'numeric' and 'aspect_ratio' not in m:
                b = m['nominal_section_width'].split('.')[-1][-1] == '5'
                m['aspect_ratio'] = '82' if b else '92'
            return _format_tyre_dimensions(m)
        except (AttributeError, schema.SchemaError):
            pass
    raise ValueError('Invalid tyre code: %s', tyre_code)

def calculate_tyre_dimensions(tyre_code):
    import schema
    it = [
        ('iso', _re_tyre_code_iso),
        ('numeric', _re_tyre_code_numeric),
        ('pax', _re_tyre_code_pax)
    ]
    for c, _r in it:
        try:
            m = _r.match(tyre_code).groupdict()
            m['code'] = c
            if c == 'numeric' and 'aspect_ratio' not in m:
                b = m['nominal_section_width'].split('.')[-1][-1] == '5'
                m['aspect_ratio'] = '82' if b else '92'
            return _format_tyre_dimensions(m)
        except (AttributeError, schema.SchemaError):
            pass
    raise ValueError('Invalid tyre code: %s', tyre_code)

```
def _format_tyre_dimensions(data):
    schema = Schema({
        'code': str,
        'tyre_code': str,
        'nominal_section_width': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('aspect_ratio'): And(str, Use(float)),
        'rim_diameter': And(str, Use(float)),
        ...
    })
    data = schema.validate(data)
    if data['code'] == 'iso':
        data['tyre_width'] = data['nominal_section_width']
        data['tyre_height'] = data['nominal_section_width'] * data['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        data['tyre_diameter'] = data['rim_diameter'] + 2 * data['tyre_height']
    ...
    return data
```

```
        \s*

        \s*

    )

""", re.VERBOSE)
```

Then `_format_tyre_dimensions` might be:
```
def _format_tyre_dimensions(m):
    s = Schema({
        'tyre_code': str,
        'code': str,
        'nominal_section_width': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('aspect_ratio'): And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('overall_diameter'): And(str, Use(float)),
        'rim_diameter': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('construction'): str,
        Optional('profile_code'): str,
        Optional('load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index_2'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol_2'): str,
    })
    d = s.validate(m)
    if d['code'] == 'iso':
        d['tyre_height'] = d['nominal_section_width'] * d['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        d['tyre_diameter'] = d['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * d['tyre_height']
        d['tyre_width'] = d['nominal_section_width']
    elif d['code'] == 'numeric':
        d['tyre_width'] = d['nominal_section_width'] * 25.4
        d['tyre_height'] = d['tyre_width'] * d['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        d['tyre_diameter'] = d['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * d['tyre_height']
    elif d['code'] == 'pax':
        d['tyre_width'] = d['nominal_section_width']
        d['tyre_height'] = (d['overall_diameter'] - d['rim_diameter']) / 2
        d['tyre_diameter'] = d['overall_diameter']
        d['aspect_ratio'] = d['tyre_height'] / d['tyre_width'] * 100
    return d
```
This is plausible. def _format_tyre_dimensions(m):
```
    schema = Schema({
        'tyre_code': str,
        'code': str,
        'nominal_section_width': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('aspect_ratio'): And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('overall_diameter'): And(str, Use(float)),
        'rim_diameter': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index_2'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol_2'): str,
    })
    m = schema.validate(m)
    if m['code'] == 'iso':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'numeric':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * 25.4
        m['tyre_height'] = m['tyre_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'pax':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = (m['overall_diameter'] - m['rim_diameter']) / 2
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['overall_diameter']
        m['aspect_ratio'] = m['tyre_height'] / m['tyre_width'] * 100
    return m
```
But note: For PAX, `rim_diameter` is in mm (440), not inches. So no *25.4. For ISO, rim_diameter is in inches (16), so *25.4. For numeric, rim_diameter in inches (16), so *25.4. This matches.

What about `nominal_section_width` for numeric: input "6.50-16" -> 6.5 inches, tyre_width = 6.5*25.4=165.1 mm. Aspect ratio computed as 82/92. tyre_height = tyre_width * aspect_ratio / 100 = 165.1*0.82=135.382 mm. tyre_diameter = 16*25.4 + 2*135.382 = 406.4+270.764=677.164 mm. Maybe.

For PAX: tyre_width = 205, tyre_height = (640-440)/2 = 100, tyre_diameter = 640, aspect_ratio = 100/205*100 = 48.78048780487805.

```
_re_tyre_code_iso = re.compile(r"""

        (?P<width>\d{3})
        /
        (?P<aspect_ratio>\d{2})
        \s*

        \s*
        (?P<rim_diameter>\d{2,3})
        \s*
        (?P<load_index>\d{2,3})
        \s*
        (?P<speed_symbol>[A-Z])
    )
    \s*$
""", re.VERBOSE)
```

```
def _format_tyre_dimensions(m):
    s = Schema({
        'tyre_code': str,
        'code': str,
        'nominal_section_width': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('aspect_ratio'): And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('overall_diameter'): And(str, Use(float)),
        'rim_diameter': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('construction'): str,
        Optional('profile_code'): str,
        Optional('load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index_2'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol_2'): str,
    })
    m = s.validate(m)
    if m['code'] == 'iso':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'numeric':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * 25.4
        m['tyre_height'] = m['tyre_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'pax':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = (m['overall_diameter'] - m['rim_diameter']) / 2
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['overall_diameter']
        m['aspect_ratio'] = m['tyre_height'] / m['tyre_width'] * 100
    return m
```

```
def _format_tyre_dimensions(m):
    s = Schema({
        'tyre_code': str,
        'code': str,
        'nominal_section_width': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('aspect_ratio'): And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('overall_diameter'): And(str, Use(float)),
        'rim_diameter': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('construction'): str,
        Optional('profile_code'): str,
        Optional('load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('speed_symbol'): str,

        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index_2'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol_2'): str,

    if m['code'] == 'iso':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']

        m['tyre_height'] = m['tyre_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
```

```
def _format_tyre_dimensions(m):
    s = Schema({
        'tyre_code': str,
        'code': str,
        'nominal_section_width': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('aspect_ratio'): And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('overall_diameter'): And(str, Use(float)),
        'rim_diameter': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('construction'): str,
        Optional('profile_code'): str,
        Optional('load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index_2'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol_2'): str,
    })
    m = s.validate(m)
    if m['code'] == 'iso':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'numeric':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * 25.4
        m['tyre_height'] = m['tyre_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'pax':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = (m['overall_diameter'] - m['rim_diameter']) / 2
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['overall_diameter']
        m['aspect_ratio'] = m['tyre_height'] / m['tyre_width'] * 100
    return m
```
This is exactly what I can imagine. The output would be as above.

```
def validate(self, data):
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        data = dict(data)
        for key, value in self._schema.items():
            if key in data:
                data[key] = value.validate(data[key])
            elif isinstance(key, Optional):
                ...
        return data
```
It copies data via `dict(data)` preserving order. It doesn't reorder. So yes.

```
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

import re
from schema import Schema, Optional, And, Use

_re_tyre_code_iso = re.compile(r"""
    ^\s*
    (?P<tyre_code>
        (?P<nominal_section_width>\d{3})
        /
        (?P<aspect_ratio>\d{2})
        \s*
        (?P<construction>[A-Z])
        \s*
        (?P<rim_diameter>\d{2,3})
        \s*
        (?P<load_index>\d{2,3})
        \s*
        (?P<speed_symbol>[A-Z])
    )
    \s*$
""", re.VERBOSE)

_re_tyre_code_numeric = re.compile(r"""
    ^\s*
    (?P<tyre_code>
        (?P<nominal_section_width>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)
        [-x]
        (?P<rim_diameter>\d+(?:\.\d+)?)
        \s*
        (?P<construction>[A-Z]?)
        \s*
        (?P<load_index>\d{2,3})
        \s*
        (?P<speed_symbol>[A-Z])
    )
    \s*$
""", re.VERBOSE)

_re_tyre_code_pax = re.compile(r"""
    ^\s*
    (?P<tyre_code>
        (?P<nominal_section_width>\d{3})
        -
        (?P<overall_diameter>\d{3})
        \s*
        (?P<construction>[A-Z])
        \s*
        (?P<rim_diameter>\d{3})
        \s*
        (?P<profile_code>[A-Z])
        \s*
        (?P<load_index>\d{2,3})
        \s*
        (?P<speed_symbol>[A-Z])
        (?:\s*\((?P<alternative_load_index>\d{2,3})\s*(?P<alternative_speed_symbol>[A-Z])(?:,\s*(?P<alternative_load_index_2>\d{2,3})\s*(?P<alternative_speed_symbol_2>[A-Z]))?\))?
    )
    \s*$
""", re.VERBOSE)
```

Let's look at the code snippet carefully:
```
def calculate_tyre_dimensions(tyre_code):
    
    import schema
    it = [
        ('iso', _re_tyre_code_iso),
        ('numeric', _re_tyre_code_numeric),
        ('pax', _re_tyre_code_pax)
    ]
    for c, _r in it:
        try:
            m = _r.match(tyre_code).groupdict()
            m['code'] = c
            if c == 'numeric' and 'aspect_ratio' not in m:
                b = m['nominal_section_width'].split('.')[-1][-1] == '5'
                m['aspect_ratio'] = '82' if b else '92'
            return _format_tyre_dimensions(m)
        except (AttributeError, schema.SchemaError):
            pass
    raise ValueError('Invalid tyre code: %s', tyre_code)
```
The `_format_tyre_dimensions(m)` is called with m containing `code` and all regex groups. It likely validates m using schema, then calculates dimensions, then returns m. The name `_format_tyre_dimensions` suggests it formats the dimensions, not necessarily renames keys. The code sets `m['code'] = c` specifically because `_format_tyre_dimensions` needs to know the type. It could also be used in output. So final output likely includes `code`.

```
def _format_tyre_dimensions(m):
    s = Schema({
        'tyre_code': str,
        'code': str,
        'nominal_section_width': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('aspect_ratio'): And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('overall_diameter'): And(str, Use(float)),
        'rim_diameter': And(str, Use(float)),
        Optional('construction'): str,
        Optional('profile_code'): str,
        Optional('load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol'): str,
        Optional('alternative_load_index_2'): And(str, Use(int)),
        Optional('alternative_speed_symbol_2'): str,
    })
    m = s.validate(m)
    if m['code'] == 'iso':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'numeric':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width'] * 25.4
        m['tyre_height'] = m['tyre_width'] * m['aspect_ratio'] / 100
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['rim_diameter'] * 25.4 + 2 * m['tyre_height']
    elif m['code'] == 'pax':
        m['tyre_width'] = m['nominal_section_width']
        m['tyre_height'] = (m['overall_diameter'] - m['rim_diameter']) / 2
        m['tyre_diameter'] = m['overall_diameter']
        m['aspect_ratio'] = m['tyre_height'] / m['tyre_width'] * 100
    return m
```
I don't recall any rounding. The calculations are straightforward. The output for ISO has 631.9 exactly because 16*25.4=406.4 and 2*112.75=225.5, sum 631.9. No rounding needed. For numeric, values might have many decimals, but the library might not care. For PAX, aspect_ratio has many decimals. If they wanted rounding, they would likely round all calculated values. The code snippet doesn't show rounding, but it's hidden in `_format_tyre_dimensions`. We need know.
